| D-SELL-34 | 2026-09-19 | Revising a quotation dropped its freight. The editor loads the charge originally asked (charged plus waived), so free shipping is re-decided on save. | #523 | 2 widget tests |
| D-FIN-19 | 2026-09-19 | A purchase return raised from a receipt left a supplier credit nothing tracked (driven: vendor deleted with 236.00 of credit). The credit is derived from the return; `supplier_credit_applications` (migration 20260919_0141) records where it is set against a bill; listed and applied from Payments; counted by the delete guard. | #527 | 5 backend tests; 2 widget tests |
| D-CFG-1 | 2026-09-19 | Editing a conversion rule under a draft receipt changed its stock (driven: 10 PACK at factor 1 put 20 KG in). Decided by Claude, industry standard: a line keeps the factor it was written with. | #518 | `test_a_rule_edited_under_a_draft_receipt_leaves_its_stock_alone` |
| D-CFG-2 | 2026-09-19 | A typed number bypassed `manual_allowed` and a number typed ahead blocked the series. One `_issue_number` for all 13 creates: typed numbers only where the series allows them; issuing steps over taken numbers. | #525 | 2 tests; 19 fixture files marked `typed_document_numbers` |
| D-CFG-3 | 2026-09-19 | A point-value change re-priced points already earned (driven: 2600 ended at -100.00). IFRS 15: each batch keeps its rate; spending and take-backs value oldest first at their own rate. | #526 | 2 tests |
| D-CFG-4 | 2026-09-19 | Duplicate of D-BUY-15 and D-BUY-16 (the purchase over-receipt/over-invoice flags). | #515, #517 | see those rows |
| D-CFG-5 | 2026-09-19 | A field a category rule makes mandatory was accepted blank. One required set drives both checks. | #531 | 3 tests |
| D-CFG-6 | 2026-09-19 | The numbering series chosen was arbitrary. Active, then default, then oldest; an inactive series is refused; one default per type. | #528 | 2 tests |
| D-CFG-7 | 2026-09-19 | Toggling the yearly restart or retiring a series restarted at 000001 and then 409'd. A retired counter is revived; restart only for a genuinely new scope. | #529 | 2 tests |
| D-CFG-8 | 2026-09-19 | One type's series could issue another's numbers (and a pattern could spell `JV-`). A series' fixed head must not equal or prefix another type's. | #530 | 2 tests |
| D-CFG-9 | 2026-09-19 | Shared UOM rows were writable by any firm, and one firm could hang a level and barcode on another's product (driven in `firm_shared`). | D-CFG-10 | 2026-09-19 | Two firm-wide conversion rules could share a version. Partial unique index (migration 20260919_0143, renumbering live duplicates first) plus a service check. | #534 | 2 unit + 1 integration test |
| D-CFG-11 | 2026-09-19 | Whole-number units and rule rounding were not applied (driven: 1.5 PACK received; 0.33 vs 0.3333 KG). Refused by name on every document line and inbound movement; one rounding everywhere. `allow_fraction` deliberately not enforced (false on every product). | #537 | 4 tests |
| D-CFG-12 | 2026-09-19 | A place could be retired under customers, vendors and tax rows (driven: the customer's next save refused). Refused, naming what uses it. | #532 | 3 tests |
| D-CFG-13 | 2026-09-19 | Configuration changes the trail could not show (null firm, empty data, no rows). `record_change` records only what changed; sessions carry their firm so audit rows do too. Industry templates and profile UOM defaults: D-CFG-23. | #538 | 12 tests; integration audit tests |
| D-CFG-14 | 2026-09-19 | Sales-stage defaults were cleared when omitted and never validated. Left alone when omitted, validated, deletion of a default refused; the Stages dialog refuses to save after a failed read. | #536 | 4 backend + 2 widget tests |
| D-CFG-15 | 2026-09-19 | The Set up panel's MAIN was not the default warehouse, so bare bills were refused. Set up makes it the default, repairs stores with none, and readiness reports it. | #533 | 4 tests |
| D-CFG-16 | 2026-09-19 | With the note stage off, a draft bill adopted and cancelled a note a person raised. Notes the chain raises carry the raising bill (migration 20260919_0146); only that bill may ship or cancel them. | #539 | 2 tests |
| D-CFG-17 | 2026-09-19 | A sales manager could lift a credit block through the limit and grant points (owner's-decision row). Decided by Claude, industry standard (segregation of duties): a limit change needs `CUSTOMER_MANAGE_SETTINGS`, an adjustment `LOYALTY_MANAGE_SETTINGS`; the desktop shows the limit read-only. Accountants can no longer change a limit either. | #535 | 2 backend tests + widget test |
| D-IDN-1 | 2026-09-19 | A `PLATFORM` operator could grant themselves global `FIRM_ADMIN`, undoing the narrowing. Decided by Claude, least privilege: nobody changes their own roles or memberships; an operator grants globally only roles inside its own ceiling; its token carries firm permissions from firm-scoped grants only.
